    ```python
    import json
    
    def run_script(term):
        cmd = [
            "python", "bank-derivative-quoting/scripts/opt_structured_swap_query.py",
            "--user-id", "test_user",
            "--currency-pair", "USDCNY",
            "--settle-purchase", "settle",

        return result.stdout

    results = []
    for term in terms:
        output = run_script(term)
        try:
            data = json.loads(output)
            data["term"] = term
            results.append(data)

            results.append({"term": term, "error": output})
    ```

    ```python
    import subprocess
    import json
    
    def run_dcd_query(term):
        # 构建命令行参数
        cmd = [
            "python", "bank-derivative-quoting/scripts/dcd_query.py",
            "--user_id", "10086", # 模拟 user_id，实际从 memory 获取
            "--term", term,
            "--settle_purchase", "settle",
            "--default-currency"
        ]
        
        try:
            # 执行脚本
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            if result.returncode != 0:
                return f"Error executing for {term}: {result.stderr}"
            return result.stdout
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Exception for {term}: {str(e)}"

    results = {
        "1M": run_dcd_query("1M"),
    ```
